882 entries, research to research-feishu-report-cocreate. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **research**: Close an external or repo-recorded knowledge gap with primary-source findings or a questionnaire for one person. Use when asked to research a topic, search the web or official docs, gather API facts from documentation, use read-only git history for when something shipped or what a changelog says, write a cited research note, or prepare questions for someone who holds a needed answer. Not for how this repo runs today (codebase-learner), why code changed or failed (trace), or choosing a technology stack (super-tech-blueprint). ([pahlevikun/levistack](https://github.com/pahlevikun/levistack/tree/HEAD/skills/knowledge/research))
- **research**: Web research with cost-graded tool ladder — WebSearch (discovery) → WebFetch (targeted, cached) → Playwright MCP (when blocked / needs JS / auth). Reconnaissance-then-action for browser interaction. Structured sources table, hallucination guard ("retract claims you can't quote"), depth modes (--quick 3 sources / --deep 10+). Use when user says "research", "find info on", "look up", or invokes "/research &lt;query&gt;". ([ParkAndre/project-starter-template](https://github.com/ParkAndre/project-starter-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research))
- **research**: Deep multi-agent research on any topic. Use when the user asks to research a topic, investigate a question, do a literature survey, or explore a domain in depth. TRIGGER when: user says "research", "investigate", "deep dive", "survey", "explore", "what does the literature say", "find out about". ([patforna/core-skills](https://github.com/patforna/core-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research))

- **research-audit-topic**: Audit a single research topic across every lens — consistency, coverage, quality, coherence, and graphics — in one pass, inserting AUDIT directives and advancing the topic to `audited`.
